BY DIRECTOR OF TOWN PLANNING
Maharashtra Regional Planning and Town Planning Act, 1966
Order
No. Preliminary TPS No.1/Pachora/Dist. Jalgaon/Sec. 86 (1)(a)/TPV-4/4847/2025.– Whereas, the draft Town
Planning Scheme No. 1, Pachora submitted by the Pachora Municipal Council vide it’s letter No.1552, dated
14th October 2024 (hereinafter referred to as “the said Draft Scheme”) has been sanctioned by the Director
of Town Planning, Maharashtra State, Pune vide Notiﬁ cation No. TPS /Pachora/Sanction/ Sanction 68(2)/
TPV-4/6346, dated 3rd December 2024 under sub-section (2) of the Section 68 of the Maharashtra Regional
Planning and Town Planning Act, 1966 (hereinafter referred to as “the said Act”) said Act, as per the power
delegated to them under the provisions of Section 151 (1) of the the said Act ;
and whereas, the Director of Town Planning, Maharashtra State, Pune vide Notiﬁ cation No. TPS Pachora/
Arbitrator/Section 72(1)/TPV-4/6608, dated 16th December 2024, issued under the provisions of sub-section (1)
of Section 72 of the said Act, and as per the power delegated to them under the provisions of Section 151
(1) of the said Act, has appointed Shri. Manohar Bharga, Retired Assistant Director of Town Planning, as the
Arbitrator in respect of the said Draft Scheme (hereinafter referred to as “the said Arbitrator”) ;
and whereas, the said Arbitrator, after following the prescribed procedure as required under the provisions
of sub-section (3) and (4) of Section 72 of the said Act, has drawn up in the prescribed form, the Preliminary
Town Planning Scheme No. 1, Pachora in accordance with the said sanctioned Draft Scheme ;
and whereas, the said Arbitrator has submitted, the Preliminary Town Planning Scheme No. 1, Pachora under
the provisions of sub-section (5) of Section 72 of the said Act, to the Director of Town Planning, Maharashtra
State, Pune for sanction vide letter No. 27, dated 30th June 2025 (hereinafter referred to as the “said Draft
Preliminary Town Planning Scheme”) ;
and whereas, in accordance with the provisions of sub-section (1) (a) of Section 86 of the said Act, the
said Preliminary Town Planning Scheme is required to be sanctioned within a period of two months from the date
of receipt of the preliminary scheme from the Arbitrator under the provisions of Section 72 (5) of the said Act ;
and whereas, the Government of Maharashtra in Urban Development Department vide it’s Notiﬁ cation,
No. TPS-1813/3211/CR-508/UD-13, dated 1st December 2016, has delegated the powers exercisable by it,
under Section 86 (1) and (2) of the said Act, in respect of all Municipal Councils and Nagarpanchayats, to the
Director of Town Planning, Maharashtra State, Pune ;
and whereas, the Director of Town Planning, Maharashtra State, Pune after making necessary enquiries is
of the opinion that, it is necessary to sanction the said Draft Preliminary Town Planning Scheme subject to the
modiﬁ cations as proposed by the said Arbitrator, more particularly mentioned in the Schedule 1 appended hereto ;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers delegated to him under the provisions of sub- section (1) and
(2) of Section 86 of the said Act, and all other powers enabling in this behalf, the Director of Town Planning,
Maharashtra State, Pune, hereby –
(a) sanctions the said Preliminary Town Planning Scheme No. 1, Pachora subject to the modiﬁ cations as
proposed by the said Arbitrator, more particularly mentioned in the Schedule-1 appended hereto.
(b) directs that this Notiﬁ cation and the said Preliminary Town Planning Scheme No. 1, Pachora as
sanctioned shall be kept open for inspection by the public for a period of six months during oﬃ ce hours on all
working days in the head oﬃ ce of the Municipal Council, Pachora.
(c) Directs that the copies of the said Preliminary scheme or extracts therefrom certiﬁ ed to be correct shall
be available for sale to the public on application at a reasonable price at the oﬃ ce of the Municipal Council
Pachora, and
(d) ﬁ xes 1st September 2025 to be the date on which all the liabilities created shall take eﬀ ect and the
said Preliminary Town Planning Scheme No. 1, Pachora as sanctioned shall come into force.
This notiﬁ cation shall also be available for inspection of general public during oﬃ ce hours on all working
days for the period of one month in the following oﬃ ces –
(i) Arbitrator, Town Planning Scheme No. 1, Pachora.
(ii) Joint Director of Town Planning, Nashik Division, Nashik.
(iii) Assistant Director of Town Planning, Jalgaon Branch, Dist. Jalgaon.
This Notiﬁ cation is made available on the www.dtp.maharashtra.gov.in website of Director of Town
Planning, Maharashtra State, Pune.
